# ERP SYSTEM Functional Requirements Document — Phase 3
## 1. Scope and evidence standard
This document contains 2963 traceable requirements: 8 platform requirements, 666 module requirements, module feature-facet requirements, and cross-cutting validation/business/security/operations/report requirements. Every module requirement is linked to a manifest and every facet requirement is emitted only when the corresponding artifact class exists.
A requirement marked **runtime verification required** means source evidence establishes the implementation surface, but not that a particular deployment enables or successfully executes it.
## 2. Requirement families
| Family | Rule | Evidence policy |
|---|---|---|
| FR | Platform functionality | Core entry points and services |
| FR-MOD | One per manifest-backed addon | Manifest path and dependency declarations |
| FR-FEAT | Artifact-backed module facet | Models/controllers/UI/security/jobs/reports/tests |
| FR-VAL | Validation behavior | ORM decorators and validation mechanisms |
| FR-BR | Business/platform rules | Dependency, transaction, or declared processing rules |
| FR-SEC | Security behavior | Route and access-control implementation |
| FR-OPS / FR-RPT | Operations and reports | Scheduled/report declarations and services |
## 3. Complete requirement register
The complete register is machine-readable in `Functional_Requirements.csv`.

## 4. Positive and negative scenario model
For each requirement, the positive scenario is the stated trigger under its preconditions and the expected output in the register. Negative scenarios are explicit: missing dependencies, invalid manifests, failed validations, denied authorization, invalid input, database errors, external provider failures, and report/rendering failures must produce the implementation-defined error path. Exact error text and rollback behavior are not inferred where no runtime execution evidence exists.
